  <p class="note">슬기로운골프생활 시험판입니다. 가입이나 서버 저장 없이 이 기기 브라우저에만 남습니다. 골프 유형 검사 응답만 검사 서버에 저장됩니다. 정산은 계산만 해 주고, 돈은 주고받지 않습니다.</p>
</div>

<script>
/* ---------- 골프 유형 검사 (GRAS ERFA) — 문항과 채점은 검사 서버에만 있다 ---------- */
(function(){
  var GRAS_API = "https://script.google.com/macros/s/AKfycbz4aGblc6lrfqolwbt6x9imyHetDBVgHcPnb5SeZJsmbqnmnkEciMFo1xt3NoIPcwDJ/exec";               /* 배포한 Apps Script 웹 앱 주소(…/exec) */
  var APP_VERSION = "round-v69";
  var LKEY = "gras_app_v1";

  function $(id){return document.getElementById(id);}
  function esc(s){return String(s==null?"":s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return {"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[c];});}
  function load(){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LKEY)||"{}")||{};}catch(e){return {};}}
  function store(){try{localStorage.setItem(LKEY,JSON.stringify(G));}catch(e){}}

  var G = load();                  /* {pass, nick, last} — 이 기기에만 */
  var Q = null, A = {};            /* 문항 목록, 응답 */

  var ERR = {
    PASSCODE:"테스트 암호가 맞지 않아요. 받은 암호를 다시 확인해 주세요.",
    CONSENT:"동의에 체크해야 검사할 수 있어요.",
    AGE:"만 19세 이상만 검사할 수 있어요.",
    ANSWERS:"답하지 않은 문제가 있어요.",
    SERVER:"검사 서버에서 오류가 났어요. 잠시 뒤 다시 해 주세요.",
    BAD_REQUEST:"요청이 올바르지 않아요. 앱을 새로고침한 뒤 다시 해 주세요.",
    NO_SERVER:"검사 서버를 준비하고 있어요. 조금만 기다려 주세요.",
    NETWORK:"검사 서버에 연결하지 못했어요. 인터넷 연결을 확인해 주세요. Claude 미리보기 화면에서는 연결이 막혀 있어서, 받은 시험판 주소에서 열어야 해요."
  };

  function api(body){
    if(!GRAS_API)return Promise.resolve({ok:false,error:"NO_SERVER"});
    return fetch(GRAS_API,{method:"POST",headers:{"Content-Type":"text/plain;charset=utf-8"},body:JSON.stringify(body)})
      .then(function(r){return r.json();})
      .catch(function(){return {ok:false,error:"NETWORK"};});
  }
  function msg(id,text,ok){var m=$(id);m.textContent=text||"";m.className="gx-msg"+(ok?" ok":"");}
  function busy(btn,on,label){btn.disabled=on;if(label)btn.textContent=label;}

  function show(step){$("gxStart").hidden=step!=="start";$("gxForm").hidden=step!=="form";$("gxResult").hidden=step!=="result";}

  window.GRAS_OPEN=function(){
    if(!$("gxPass").value&&G.pass)$("gxPass").value=G.pass;
    if(!$("gxNick").value&&G.nick)$("gxNick").value=G.nick;
    if(Q&&$("gxForm").hidden===false)return;
    if(G.last){renderResult(G.last);show("result");}else show("start");
  };

  /* ---------- 1. 시작 ---------- */
  function start(btn){
    if(!$("gxAdult").checked){msg("gxStartMsg",ERR.AGE);return;}
    if(!$("gxConsent").checked){msg("gxStartMsg",ERR.CONSENT);return;}
    G.pass=$("gxPass").value.trim();G.nick=$("gxNick").value.trim();store();
    busy(btn,true,"불러오는 중…");msg("gxStartMsg","");
    api({action:"questions",passcode:G.pass}).then(function(r){
      busy(btn,false,"검사 시작");
      if(!r.ok){msg("gxStartMsg",ERR[r.error]||ERR.SERVER);return;}
      Q=r;A={};renderQs();show("form");window.scrollTo(0,0);
    });
  }

  /* ---------- 2. 문항 ---------- */
  function renderQs(){
    var s=Q.scale;
    $("gxQs").innerHTML=Q.questions.map(function(q,i){
      var btns="";for(var v=s.min;v<=s.max;v++)btns+='<button type="button" data-gq="'+esc(q.id)+'" data-gv="'+v+'" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="'+v+'점">'+v+'</button>';
      return '<li class="gx-q" id="gx-'+esc(q.id)+'"><p><span>'+(i+1)+'</span>'+esc(q.text)+'</p><div class="gx-scale" role="group" aria-label="'+(i+1)+'번 답">'+btns+'</div><div class="gx-ends"><span>'+esc(s.minLabel)+'</span><span>'+esc(s.maxLabel)+'</span></div></li>';
    }).join("");
    progress();msg("gxFormMsg","");
  }
  function progress(){
    var n=Object.keys(A).length,t=Q.questions.length;
    $("gxCount").textContent=n+" / "+t;$("gxBarFill").style.width=(n/t*100)+"%";
  }
  function pick(id,v){
    A[id]=v;
    var li=$("gx-"+id);li.classList.remove("miss");
    li.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(function(b){b.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(+b.dataset.gv===v));});
    progress();
    if(Object.keys(A).length===Q.questions.length)msg("gxFormMsg","");
  }
  function submit(btn){
    var miss=Q.questions.filter(function(q){return A[q.id]==null;});
    if(miss.length){
      miss.forEach(function(q){$("gx-"+q.id).classList.add("miss");});
      msg("gxFormMsg","답 안 한 문제가 "+miss.length+"개 있어요. 빨간 테두리를 확인해 주세요.");
      $("gx-"+miss[0].id).scrollIntoView({block:"center"});return;
    }
    busy(btn,true,"채점하는 중…");msg("gxFormMsg","");
    api({action:"score",passcode:G.pass,consent:true,adult:true,nickname:G.nick,app_version:APP_VERSION,answers:A}).then(function(r){
      busy(btn,false,"결과 보기");
      if(!r.ok){msg("gxFormMsg",ERR[r.error]||ERR.SERVER);return;}
      r.nick=G.nick;r.at=new Date().toISOString();
      G.last=r;store();renderResult(r);show("result");window.scrollTo(0,0);
    });
  }

  /* ---------- 3. 결과 ---------- */
  function list(arr){return '<ul class="gx-list">'+(arr||[]).map(function(x){return '<li>'+esc(x)+'</li>';}).join("")+'</ul>';}
  function renderResult(r){
    var p=r.profile||{},ch=p.chemistry||{},fun=p.fun||{};
    var axes=(r.axes||[]).map(function(a){
      var pos=(a.max-a.score)/(a.max-a.min)*100;
      return '<div class="gx-axis"><div class="lab">'+esc(a.band==="middle"?"반반, 살짝 "+a[a.side].name:a[a.side].name)+' <span>'+a.score+'점</span></div>'+
        '<div class="gx-track" aria-hidden="true"><i class="gx-dot" style="left:'+pos.toFixed(1)+'%"></i></div>'+
        '<div class="ends"><span><i><b>'+esc(a.left.code)+'</b> '+esc(a.left.name)+'</i>'+(a.left.easy?'<small>'+esc(a.left.easy)+'</small>':'')+'</span><span class="r"><i>'+esc(a.right.name)+' <b>'+esc(a.right.code)+'</b></i>'+(a.right.easy?'<small>'+esc(a.right.easy)+'</small>':'')+'</span></div></div>';
    }).join("");
    var chem=[["fantasy","환상의 케미"],["surprise","의외의 케미"],["chaos","환장의 케미"],["twin","쌍둥이"]].filter(function(k){return ch[k[0]];}).map(function(k){
      return '<div><small>'+k[1]+'</small><b>'+esc(ch[k[0]].code)+'</b><span>'+esc(ch[k[0]].name)+'</span>'+(ch[k[0]].easy?'<small>'+esc(ch[k[0]].easy)+'</small>':'')+'</div>';
    }).join("");
    var practice=(p.practice||[]).map(function(x){return '<div><b>'+esc(x.title)+'</b><span>'+esc(x.text)+'</span></div>';}).join("");
    $("gxResult").innerHTML=
      '<div class="gx-hero"><small>'+(r.nick?esc(r.nick)+'님의 ':'')+'골프 스타일</small><div class="gx-code">'+esc(r.code_display)+'</div><h2>'+esc(r.type_name)+'</h2>'+(r.easy_name?'<div class="gx-easy">'+esc(r.easy_name)+'</div>':'')+'<p>'+esc(p.character_line)+'</p></div>'+
      '<section class="card"><h2>나는 어느 쪽?</h2><div class="gx-axes">'+axes+'</div><p class="gx-fine">점이 가운데에 가까우면 양쪽을 다 가진 편이에요.</p></section>'+
      '<p class="gx-exec"><small>다음 라운드에 이것만 기억하세요</small>'+esc(p.execution_line)+'</p>'+
      '<section class="card"><h2>내가 잘하는 것</h2>'+list(p.strengths)+'</section>'+
      '<section class="card"><h2>이럴 때 조심</h2>'+list(p.risk_scenes)+'</section>'+
      '<section class="card"><h2>이렇게 해 보세요</h2>'+list(p.common_improvements)+'<div class="gx-practice">'+practice+'</div></section>'+
      '<section class="card"><h2>라운드 끝나고 체크해 보세요</h2>'+list(p.checkpoints)+'</section>'+
      '<section class="card"><h2>캐디가 본 나</h2><dl class="gx-fun">'+
        (fun.caddie_view?'<dt>캐디 한마디</dt><dd>'+esc(fun.caddie_view)+'</dd>':'')+
        (fun.danger_button?'<dt>나를 흔드는 것</dt><dd>'+esc(fun.danger_button)+'</dd>':'')+
        (fun.forbidden_word?'<dt>하면 안 되는 말</dt><dd>“'+esc(fun.forbidden_word)+'”</dd>':'')+
        (fun.combo?'<dt>조심할 동반자</dt><dd>'+esc(fun.combo)+'</dd>':'')+
      '</dl></section>'+
      (chem?'<section class="card"><h2>동반자 케미</h2><div class="gx-chem">'+chem+'</div><p class="gx-fine">재미로 보는 궁합이에요. 실제 사이를 정해 주는 건 아니에요.</p></section>':'')+
      '<div class="share"><button type="button" class="btn btn-flag" data-gx="copy">동반자에게 보낼 문구 복사</button><button type="button" class="btn btn-plain" data-gx="again">다시 검사하기</button></div>'+
      '<textarea class="copybox" id="gxCopy" readonly hidden aria-label="복사할 문구"></textarea>'+
      '<p class="gx-fine">'+esc(p.disclaimer)+'</p>'+
      '<p class="gx-fine">결과 '+esc(r.result_version||"")+' · 번호 '+esc(r.response_id||"")+(r.saved===false?' · 응답 저장에 실패했어요(결과는 정상이에요)':'')+'</p>';
  }
  function shareText(r){
    var ch=(r.profile||{}).chemistry||{},L=[];
    L.push("[골프 유형 검사] 나는 "+r.code_display+" "+r.type_name+(r.easy_name?" ("+r.easy_name+")":""));
    if((r.profile||{}).execution_line)L.push("내 한 문장: "+r.profile.execution_line);
    if(ch.fantasy)L.push("환상의 케미: "+ch.fantasy.code+" "+ch.fantasy.name);
    if(ch.chaos)L.push("환장의 케미: "+ch.chaos.code+" "+ch.chaos.name);
    L.push(((r.profile||{}).share_card||{}).cta||"너 골프 유형 뭐 나왔어?");
    return L.join("\n");
  }
  function copy(){
    var t=shareText(G.last),box=$("gxCopy");
    function fallback(){box.hidden=false;box.value=t;box.focus();box.select();}
    try{navigator.clipboard.writeText(t).then(function(){box.hidden=true;flash("복사했어요. 단톡방에 붙여 넣으세요");},fallback);}catch(e){fallback();}
  }
  function flash(text){var t=document.querySelector(".toast");if(!t){t=document.createElement("div");t.className="toast";t.setAttribute("role","status");document.body.appendChild(t);}t.textContent=text;t.hidden=false;clearTimeout(flash.h);flash.h=setTimeout(function(){t.hidden=true;},2200);}

  document.addEventListener("click",function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest("button");if(!b)return;var d=b.dataset;
    if(d.gq){pick(d.gq,+d.gv);return;}
    if(d.gx==="start"){start(b);return;}
    if(d.gx==="submit"){submit(b);return;}
    if(d.gx==="copy"){copy();return;}
    if(d.gx==="again"){Q=null;A={};show("start");window.scrollTo(0,0);return;}
  });
})();
</script>

      stops:{coffee:{on:false,where:"course"},gimbap:{on:false,where:"home"},store:{on:false,where:"home"},gas:{on:false,where:"home"},meal:{on:false,where:"course"}},
      bet:{on:false,game:"tadang",amt:{},bp:{tie3:true,tie4:true,triple:true,par3dbl:true,birdie:true},bpWhen:"next",bonus:{birdie:{mode:"stroke",v:1},putt:{mode:"stroke",v:3},shot:{mode:"stroke",v:5},hio:{mode:"stroke",v:10}},eg:{},pk:{},dr:{},joker:"bogey",drawMode:"app",vtie:"carry",stack:"cap4",hrank:2,skip:{first:"on",last:"on"},nearMode:"carry",order:null,vg:{},hz:{},near:{},dc:{},ab:{},gal:[],uc:{}},age19:false,
      par:DEFAULT_PAR.slice(),scores:[0,1,2,3].map(function(){return Array(18).fill(null);}),hole:0,sample:false};
  }
  function sample(){
    var s=blank();
    s.course="장수CC";s.sample=true;s.players=["나","민수","지훈","현우"];s.move="drive";
    s.pick={"1":{on:true,place:"래미안 정문",extra:10}};s.first=true;s.buffer=50;
    s.stops.coffee.on=true;s.bet=Object.assign(s.bet,{on:true,game:"tadang",amt:{tadang:{unit:1000}}});s.age19=true;
    var rows=[[5,4,3,6,5,4,4],[4,5,4,5,4,5,3],[6,4,4,7,5,4,3],[4,4,2,5,4,4,4]];
    rows.forEach(function(r,i){r.forEach(function(v,h){s.scores[i][h]=v;});});
    s.hole=7;return s;
  }
  function load(){
    try{var t=localStorage.getItem(KEY);if(t){var o=JSON.parse(t),b=blank();
      Object.keys(b).forEach(function(k){if(o[k]===undefined)o[k]=b[k];});
      o.stops=Object.assign({},b.stops,o.stops||{});o.traffic=0;if(o.stops&&o.stops.meal&&o.stops.meal.where==="home")o.stops.meal.where="course";var ob=o.bet||{};o.bet=Object.assign({},b.bet,ob);o.bet.vg=o.bet.vg||{};if(ob.bp&&ob.bp.tie4===undefined)o.bet.bp=Object.assign({},ob.bp,{tie4:!!ob.bp.tie3});o.bet.hz=o.bet.hz||{};var sk0=o.bet.skip||{};o.bet.skip={first:sk0.first===true?"off":(sk0.first||"on"),last:sk0.last===true?"off":(sk0.last||"on")};if([2,"turn","manual"].indexOf(o.bet.hrank)<0)o.bet.hrank=2;o.bet.near=o.bet.near||{};if(!ob.bp){o.bet.bp={tie3:ob.tie!==false,tie4:ob.tie!==false,triple:true,par3dbl:true,birdie:ob.birdie!=="none"};}
      if(!ob.bonus){o.bet.bonus=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(b.bet.bonus));var ob2=ob.bbonus||(ob.birdie==="cash"||ob.birdie==="extra"?ob.birdie:null);if(ob2==="cash")o.bet.bonus.birdie={mode:"cash",v:((ob.amt||{}).tadang||{}).bonus||0};else if(ob2==="none")o.bet.bonus.birdie={mode:"none",v:1};}
      o.bet.eg=o.bet.eg||{};o.bet.pk=o.bet.pk||{};o.bet.dr=o.bet.dr||{};if(o.bet.stack!=="cap2")o.bet.stack="cap4";
      if(!ob.amt){o.bet.amt={};o.bet.amt[o.bet.game||"tadang"]={unit:o.bet.unit||0,seed:o.bet.seed||0};}return o;}}catch(e){}
    return blank();
  }
  function save(){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(S));}catch(e){}}
  var S=load();
  var $=function(id){return document.getElementById(id);};
  function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return {"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[c];});}
  function act(){var a=[];S.players.forEach(function(n,i){if(i<2||String(n).trim())a.push(i);});return a;}
  function nameOf(i){var n=String(S.players[i]||"").trim();return n||(i===0?"나":"동반자 "+i);}
  function vp(n){return n>0?"+"+n:String(n);}
  var toMin=function(h){var p=String(h||"07:00").split(":");return (+p[0])*60+(+p[1]);};
  var fmt=function(m){m=((Math.round(m)%1440)+1440)%1440;var h=Math.floor(m/60),mm=m%60;return (h<10?"0":"")+h+":"+(mm<10?"0":"")+mm;};
  var kakao=function(q){return "https://map.kakao.com/link/search/"+encodeURIComponent(q);};
  var naver=function(q){return "https://map.naver.com/p/search/"+encodeURIComponent(q);};
  function toast(msg){var t=document.querySelector(".toast");if(!t){t=document.createElement("div");t.className="toast";t.setAttribute("role","status");document.body.appendChild(t);}t.textContent=msg;t.hidden=false;clearTimeout(toast.t);toast.t=setTimeout(function(){t.hidden=true;},1800);}
  function copy(txt,boxId,okMsg){
    var fb=$(boxId);
    var fail=function(){fb.value=txt;fb.hidden=false;fb.focus();fb.select();toast("자동 복사가 막혔어요. 아래 글을 길게 눌러 복사하세요.");};
    try{navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).then(function(){fb.hidden=true;toast(okMsg);},fail);}catch(e){fail();}
  }

  /* ---------- 가는 길 계산 ---------- */
  function pickers(){ // 내가 태우는 사람들(이름이 있는 동반자만)
    if(S.move!=="drive")return [];
    return act().filter(function(i){return i>0&&S.pick[i]&&S.pick[i].on;}).map(function(i){return {i:i,place:S.pick[i].place||"",extra:S.pick[i].extra||10};});
  }
  function plan(){
    var inMeal=S.stops.meal.on&&S.stops.meal.where==="inside",mealT=inMeal?STOPS.filter(function(d){return d.id==="meal";})[0].stay:0;
    var tee=toMin(S.tee),arrive=tee-S.buffer-mealT,D=Math.round(S.drive*(1+S.traffic/100));
    var on=STOPS.filter(function(d){return S.stops[d.id].on&&!(d.id==="meal"&&inMeal);});
    var home=on.filter(function(d){return S.stops[d.id].where==="home";}),near=on.filter(function(d){return S.stops[d.id].where==="course";});
    var stopTime=on.reduce(function(a,d){return a+d.stay+DETOUR;},0);
    var pk=pickers(),pickTime=pk.reduce(function(a,x){return a+x.extra+LOAD;},0);
    var depart=arrive-D-stopTime-pickTime;
    var a=home.length?Math.max(3,Math.round(D*.12)):0,c=near.length?Math.max(3,Math.round(D*.12)):0,b=D-a-c;
    var ev=[],t=depart;
    ev.push({t:t,k:"start",title:S.move==="ride"?"태워 줄 차에 타기":"집에서 출발",note:"골프장까지 "+D+"분"+(S.traffic?" (막힘 여유 포함)":"")+(pk.length?" · 픽업 "+pk.length+"명 +"+pickTime+"분":"")});
    pk.forEach(function(x){t+=x.extra;ev.push({t:t,k:"pick",x:x,title:nameOf(x.i)+" 픽업",note:(x.place?x.place+" · ":"")+"백 싣기 "+LOAD+"분"});t+=LOAD;});
    t+=a;home.forEach(function(d){t+=DETOUR;ev.push({t:t,k:"stop",d:d,title:d.name+" 들르기",note:d.stay+"분 · 집 근처"});t+=d.stay;});
    t+=b;near.forEach(function(d){t+=DETOUR;ev.push({t:t,k:"stop",d:d,title:d.name+" 들르기",note:d.stay+"분 · 골프장 근처"});t+=d.stay;});
    t+=c;ev.push({t:t,k:"arrive",title:"골프장 도착",note:"티오프 "+(S.buffer+mealT)+"분 전"+(inMeal?" · 클럽하우스 식사 포함":"")});
    if(inMeal)ev.push({t:arrive+15,k:"meal",title:"클럽하우스에서 식사",note:mealT+"분 · 골프장 안"});
    if(["g110","b100"].indexOf(getLevel()||"b100")>=0)ARRIVAL.forEach(function(x){var at=x.after!=null?arrive+x.after:tee-x.before;if(at>arrive&&at<tee)ev.push({t:at,k:"sub",title:x.title,note:x.note});});
    ev.push({t:tee,k:"tee",title:"티오프",note:S.course||"골프장"});
    ev.sort(function(x,y){return x.t-y.t;});
    return {depart:depart,tee:tee,arrive:arrive,D:D,ev:ev,prev:depart<0,pk:pk};
  }

  /* ---------- 정산 계산 ---------- */
  function stats(i){var tot=0,par=0,thru=0,out=0,inn=0,on=0,inN=0;for(var h=0;h<18;h++){var v=S.scores[i][h];if(v!=null){tot+=v;par+=S.par[h];thru++;if(h<9){out+=v-S.par[h];on++;}else{inn+=v-S.par[h];inN++;}}}return {tot:tot,vs:tot-par,thru:thru,out:out,inn:inn,on:on,inN:inN};}
  function stackMul(m,x,st){var v=m*x;return st==="cap2"?Math.min(v,2):(st==="cap4"?Math.min(v,4):v);}

  function amtFields(gid){if(gid==="hussein"&&S.bet.hzMode==="pot")return [["seed","한 사람이 걷는 돈 (원)","예: 100000",10000],["unit","홀 상금 (원)","예: 20000",1000]];return AMT[gid]||[];}
  function amt(k,gid){var a=(S.bet.amt||{})[gid||S.bet.game]||{};return Math.max(0,Math.floor(+a[k]||0));}
  function happyUnit(){return Math.floor(amt("seed","happy")/50);}
  function gameOf(id){for(var k=0;k<GAMES.length;k++)if(GAMES[k].id===id)return GAMES[k];return GAMES[0];}
  function lastOf(h,a){if(h<0)return [];var st=fullHole(h,a);if(!st)return [];var vs=a.map(function(i){return st[i];}),mx=Math.max.apply(null,vs),mn=Math.min.apply(null,vs);if(mx===mn)return [];return a.filter(function(i){return st[i]===mx;});}
  function inIv(L,h){return (L||[]).some(function(x){return x[0]<=h&&(x[1]==null||h<x[1]);});}
  function togIv(L,h){L=(L||[]).map(function(x){return x.slice();});var k=-1;L.forEach(function(x,j){if(x[0]<=h&&(x[1]==null||h<x[1]))k=j;});if(k<0)L.push([h,null]);else if(L[k][0]===h)L.splice(k,1);else L[k][1]=h;return L;}
  function declOf(h,a){var L=lastOf(h-1,a);if(!L.length)return null;var x=(S.bet.dc||{})[h];if(x!=null&&L.indexOf(+x)>=0)return {i:+x,auto:false};var ab=S.bet.ab||{};for(var k=0;k<L.length;k++)if(inIv(ab[L[k]],h))return {i:L[k],auto:true};return null;}
  function galM(h){var r=0;(S.bet.gal||[]).forEach(function(x){if(x[0]<=h&&(x[1]==null||h<x[1]))r=x[2]||2;});return r;}
  function galOn(h){return galM(h)>0;}
  function unitAt(h,u){var uc=S.bet.uc||{},r=u;Object.keys(uc).map(Number).sort(function(x,y){return x-y;}).forEach(function(k){if(k<=h)r=+uc[k];});return r;}
  function setGal(L,h,x){L=(L||[]).map(function(y){return y.slice();});var k=-1;L.forEach(function(y,j){if(y[0]<=h&&(y[1]==null||h<y[1]))k=j;});
    if(k<0){L.push([h,null,x]);return L;}var cx=L[k][2]||2;
    if(cx===x){if(L[k][0]===h)L.splice(k,1);else L[k][1]=h;return L;}
    if(L[k][0]===h)L[k][2]=x;else{L[k][1]=h;L.push([h,null,x]);}return L;}
  function fullHole(h,a){var st={};for(var k=0;k<a.length;k++){var v=S.scores[a[k]][h];if(v==null)return null;st[a[k]]=v;}return st;}
  function zero(a){var o={};a.forEach(function(i){o[i]=0;});return o;}
  function pairwise(a,st,mul,d){for(var x=0;x<a.length;x++)for(var y=x+1;y<a.length;y++){var p=a[x],q=a[y],v=(st[q]-st[p])*mul;d[p]+=v;d[q]-=v;}}
  function teeOrder(a){var o=(S.bet.order||[]).filter(function(i){return a.indexOf(i)>=0;});a.forEach(function(i){if(o.indexOf(i)<0)o.push(i);});return o;}
  function vegasUnit(){return Math.floor(amt("seed","vegas")/10);}
  function holeMode(h){var k=S.bet.skip||{},m=h===0?k.first:(h===17?k.last:"on");m=m||"on";if(m==="birdie"&&S.bet.game!=="tadang")m="off";return m;}
  function offHole(h){return holeMode(h)==="off";}
  function bOnly(h){return holeMode(h)==="birdie";}
  function betHoles(){return 18-(offHole(0)?1:0)-(offHole(17)?1:0);}
  function nearPrize(n,gid){gid=gid||S.bet.game;var sd=amt("seed",gid),spent=gid==="vegas"?18*2*vegasUnit():18*10*happyUnit(),p3=S.par.filter(function(x){return x===3;}).length;if(!p3)return 0;return Math.max(0,Math.floor((sd*n-spent)/p3));}
  function sticksOf(h,a){var pk=(S.bet.pk||{})[h];if(!pk)return null;var by={A:[],B:[],J:[]};for(var z=0;z<a.length;z++){var c=pk[a[z]];if(!by[c])return null;by[c].push(a[z]);}
    if(by.J.length>1)return null;if(by.J.length===0)return by.A.length===2&&by.B.length===2?[by.A,by.B]:null;
    var pair=by.A.length===2?by.A:(by.B.length===2?by.B:null),lone=by.A.length===1?by.A:(by.B.length===1?by.B:null);return pair&&lone?[pair,[lone[0],by.J[0]]]:null;}
  function jokerScore(par){var j=S.bet.joker||"bogey";return j==="four"?4:(j==="par"?par:par+1);}
  var BK=[["birdie","버디"],["putt","퍼트 이글"],["shot","샷 이글"],["hio","홀인원"]];
  function bonusKind(h,i,v,par){if(v===1)return "hio";if(v<=par-2)return S.bet.eg[h+"_"+i]==="shot"?"shot":"putt";if(v===par-1)return "birdie";return null;}
  function bonusOf(k){var b=k&&(S.bet.bonus||{})[k];return b&&b.mode!=="none"?{st:b.mode==="stroke"?Math.max(0,+b.v||0):0,cash:b.mode==="cash"?Math.max(0,Math.floor(+b.v||0)):0}:{st:0,cash:0};}
  function bkName(k){for(var z=0;z<BK.length;z++)if(BK[z][0]===k)return BK[z][1];return "";}
  function hzRule(){return S.bet.hrank==="turn"?"타순대로 돌아가며":(S.bet.hrank==="manual"?"직접 고르기":"앞 홀 2등");}
  function husseinOf(h,a){
    if(S.bet.hz[h]!=null&&a.indexOf(+S.bet.hz[h])>=0)return {i:+S.bet.hz[h],auto:false};
    var hm=S.bet.hrank;if(hm==="manual")return null;
    if(hm==="turn"){var tt=teeOrder(a);return {i:tt[h%tt.length],auto:true};}
    if(h===0)return null;var st=fullHole(h-1,a);if(!st)return null;
    var to=teeOrder(a),cmp=function(x,y){if(st[x]!==st[y])return st[x]-st[y];for(var k=h-2;k>=0;k--){var vx=S.scores[x][k],vy=S.scores[y][k];if(vx!=null&&vy!=null&&vx!==vy)return vx-vy;}return to.indexOf(x)-to.indexOf(y);};
    var ord=a.slice().sort(cmp),n=Math.min(2,a.length);return {i:ord[n-1],auto:true};
  }
  function calc(){
    var a=act(),g=S.bet,G=gameOf(g.game);if(a.length<2||G.ready===false)return null;
    if(G.max&&a.length!==G.max)return {bad:G.name+"는 "+G.max+"명이 쳐야 해요.",act:a};
    if(G.min&&a.length<G.min)return {bad:G.name+"는 "+G.min+"명 이상이어야 해요.",act:a};
    if(G.id==="vegas"&&g.drawMode==="off")return {bad:"뽑기는 오프라인으로 해요. 앱은 스코어만 적고 정산하지 않아요.",act:a};
    var unit=G.id==="happy"?happyUnit():amt("unit"),tot=zero(a),per={},played=0,skipped=[],need=[],doubled=[],extra={},offH=[];
    var carry=1,win=zero(a),last=zero(a),seed=amt("seed"),pot=seed*a.length,np=nearPrize(a.length),bank=0,fund=0,nearNeed=[],dq=0;
    for(var h=0;h<18;h++){
      if(offHole(h)){offH.push(h+1);per[h]={off:true};continue;}
      var st=fullHole(h,a);
      if(!st){if(a.some(function(i){return S.scores[i][h]!=null;}))skipped.push(h+1);continue;}
      var d=zero(a),tag="",par=S.par[h];
      if(G.id==="tadang"&&bOnly(h)){var uH=unitAt(h,unit),bk2={},tg2=[];a.forEach(function(i){bk2[i]=bonusKind(h,i,st[i],par);});
        for(var x2=0;x2<a.length;x2++)for(var y2=x2+1;y2<a.length;y2++){var p2=a[x2],q2=a[y2],B1=bonusOf(bk2[p2]),B2=bonusOf(bk2[q2]),v2=(B1.st-B2.st)*uH+B1.cash-B2.cash;d[p2]+=v2;d[q2]-=v2;}
        a.forEach(function(i){if(!bk2[i])return;var B=bonusOf(bk2[i]);tg2.push(nameOf(i)+" "+bkName(bk2[i])+(B.st?" +"+B.st+"타":"")+(B.cash?" +"+money(B.cash):""));});
        carry=1;tag="버디피만 홀"+(tg2.length?" · "+tg2.join(" · "):" · 버디 없음");
      }else if(G.id==="tadang"){
        var bpo=g.bp||{},thisH=g.bpWhen==="this",uH=unitAt(h,unit);
        var bset=a.filter(function(i){return st[i]<=par-1;}),tg=[],bk={};a.forEach(function(i){bk[i]=bonusKind(h,i,st[i],par);});
        var cnt={},trig=[];a.forEach(function(i){cnt[st[i]]=(cnt[st[i]]||0)+1;});
        if(bpo.tie3&&a.length>=3&&Object.keys(cnt).some(function(k){return cnt[k]===3;}))trig.push("3명 동타");
        var all4=bpo.tie4&&a.length>=4&&Object.keys(cnt).length===1;if(all4&&!thisH)trig.push("4명 동타");
        if(bpo.triple&&a.some(function(i){return st[i]>=par+3;}))trig.push("트리플");
        if(bpo.par3dbl&&par===3&&a.some(function(i){return st[i]>=par+2;}))trig.push("파3 더블");
        if(bpo.birdie&&bset.length)trig.push("버디 이상");
        var dcl=declOf(h,a),dn=dcl?(dcl.auto?"자동 배판(":"배판 선언(")+nameOf(dcl.i)+")":null,gl=null,gm=galM(h);if(gm){trig=[];dn=null;all4=false;}
        var capN=g.stack==="cap2"?2:4,cm=Math.max(carry,thisH&&trig.length?2:1),m=Math.min(cm*(dn?2:1),capN),fromPrev=carry>1;carry=1;if(gm){m=gm;fromPrev=false;}
        for(var x=0;x<a.length;x++)for(var y=x+1;y<a.length;y++){var p=a[x],q=a[y],BP=bonusOf(bk[p]),BQ=bonusOf(bk[q]),df=st[q]-st[p]+BP.st-BQ.st;
          var v=df*m*uH+BP.cash-BQ.cash;d[p]+=v;d[q]-=v;}
        if((g.uc||{})[h]!=null)tg.push("한 타 "+money(uH)+"으로 변경");
        if(m>1){var why=[].concat(fromPrev?[thisH?"앞 홀 4명 동타":"앞 홀 배판"]:[],thisH?trig:[],dn?[dn]:[]);if(gm)why=["갈배"];tg.push("배판 ×"+m+(why.length?"("+why.join("·")+")":""));doubled.push((h+1)+"번 ×"+m);}
        a.forEach(function(i){if(!bk[i])return;var B=bonusOf(bk[i]);tg.push(nameOf(i)+" "+bkName(bk[i])+(B.st?" +"+B.st+"타":"")+(B.cash?" +"+money(B.cash):""));});
        if(!thisH&&trig.length){carry=2;tg.push("다음 홀 ×"+carry+"("+trig.join("·")+")");}
        if(thisH&&all4){carry=2;tg.push("다음 홀 ×2(4명 동타)");}
        tag=tg.join(" · ");
      }else if(G.id==="scratch"){
        pairwise(a,st,unit,d);
      }else if(G.id==="vegas"){
        var tm=sticksOf(h,a);if(!tm){need.push(h+1);per[h]={need:"뽑기 결과를 넣어 주세요"};continue;}
        var pkh=g.pk[h],sc2=function(i){return pkh[i]==="J"?jokerScore(par):st[i];},sa=sc2(tm[0][0])+sc2(tm[0][1]),sb=sc2(tm[1][0])+sc2(tm[1][1]),vu=vegasUnit(),ev3=[];
        var vt=g.vtie||"carry",mul=vt==="carry"?carry:(vt==="next"&&dq>0?2:1);if(vt==="next"&&dq>0&&sa!==sb)dq--;
        if(sa!==sb){var W=sa<sb?tm[0]:tm[1],prz=vu*mul;W.forEach(function(i){d[i]+=prz;});ev3.push(W.map(nameOf).join("·")+" 승"+(mul>1?" (×"+mul+")":""));if(vt==="carry")carry=1;}
        else if(vt==="carry"){carry++;ev3.push("동점 → 다음 홀 ×"+carry);}
        else if(vt==="next"){dq++;ev3.push("동점 → 다음에 이기는 "+dq+"홀까지 2배");}
        else ev3.push("동점 · 상금 없음");
        if(par===3){bank+=np;var nw2=g.near[h];
          if(nw2==null){nearNeed.push(h+1);ev3.push("니어 고르기 전");}
          else if(+nw2>=0&&a.indexOf(+nw2)>=0){d[+nw2]+=bank;ev3.push("니어 "+nameOf(+nw2)+" "+money(bank));bank=0;}
          else if(g.nearMode==="fund"){fund+=bank;ev3.push("니어 없음 → 공금 "+money(bank));bank=0;}
          else ev3.push("니어 없음 → 다음 파3 상금 "+money(bank+np)+"(이월)");}
        a.forEach(function(i){win[i]+=d[i];});
        tag=tm[0].map(function(i){return nameOf(i)+(pkh[i]==="J"?"(조커 "+jokerScore(par)+")":"");}).join("·")+" "+sa+" : "+sb+" "+tm[1].map(function(i){return nameOf(i)+(pkh[i]==="J"?"(조커 "+jokerScore(par)+")":"");}).join("·")+" · "+ev3.join(" · ");
      }else if(G.id==="hussein"){
        var hz=husseinOf(h,a);if(!hz){need.push(h+1);per[h]={need:"후세인을 정해 주세요"};continue;}
        var others=a.filter(function(i){return i!==hz.i;}),h3=st[hz.i]*3,so=others.reduce(function(x,i){return x+st[i];},0);
        tag="후세인 "+nameOf(hz.i)+" "+st[hz.i]+"×3="+h3+" : "+so;
        if(g.hzMode==="pot"){
          if(h3!==so){var prz2=Math.min(unit*carry,pot);
            if(h3<so){d[hz.i]+=prz2;pot-=prz2;tag+=" · 후세인 혼자 "+money(prz2);}
            else{var each2=Math.floor(prz2/others.length);others.forEach(function(i){d[i]+=each2;});pot-=each2*others.length;tag+=" · 세 명 "+money(each2)+"씩";}
            if(carry>1)tag+=" (×"+carry+")";carry=1;}
          else{carry++;tag+=" · 비김 → 다음 홀 ×"+carry;}
          a.forEach(function(i){win[i]+=d[i];});
        }else{
        if(h3<so){d[hz.i]+=unit*others.length;others.forEach(function(i){d[i]-=unit;});}
        else if(h3>so){d[hz.i]-=unit*others.length;others.forEach(function(i){d[i]+=unit;});}
        }
      }else if(G.id==="happy"){
        var hcmp=function(x,y){if(st[x]!==st[y])return st[x]-st[y];for(var k=h-1;k>=0;k--){var vx=S.scores[x][k],vy=S.scores[y][k];if(vx!=null&&vy!=null&&vx!==vy)return vy-vx;}var to=teeOrder(a);return to.indexOf(x)-to.indexOf(y);};
        var ordr=a.slice().sort(hcmp),PTS=[4,3,2,1],pos=0,ev2=[];
        while(pos<ordr.length){var e=pos;while(e+1<ordr.length&&hcmp(ordr[e+1],ordr[pos])===0)e++;var sum=0;for(var q=pos;q<=e;q++)sum+=PTS[q];var each=Math.round(sum*unit/(e-pos+1));for(q=pos;q<=e;q++){d[ordr[q]]+=each;}pos=e+1;}
        if(par===3){bank+=np;var nw=g.near[h];
          if(nw==null){nearNeed.push(h+1);ev2.push("니어 고르기 전");}
          else if(+nw>=0&&a.indexOf(+nw)>=0){d[+nw]+=bank;ev2.push("니어 "+nameOf(+nw)+" "+money(bank));bank=0;}
          else if(g.nearMode==="fund"){fund+=bank;ev2.push("니어 없음 → 공금 "+money(bank));bank=0;}
          else ev2.push("니어 없음 → 다음 파3 상금 "+money(bank+np)+"(이월)");}
        a.forEach(function(i){win[i]+=d[i];});tag=ev2.join(" · ");
      }else if(G.id==="jopok"){
        var ev=[];
        a.forEach(function(i){if(win[i]<=0)return;var over=st[i]-par;
          if(over>=3||st[i]>=par*2){pot+=win[i];d[i]-=win[i];ev.push(nameOf(i)+" 다 토해냄");win[i]=0;last[i]=0;}
          else if(over===2){var back=Math.min(last[i],win[i]);if(back>0){pot+=back;d[i]-=back;win[i]-=back;ev.push(nameOf(i)+" 마지막 딴 돈 반납");}last[i]=0;}});
        var best=Math.min.apply(null,a.map(function(i){return st[i];})),top=a.filter(function(i){return st[i]===best;});
        if(top.length===1){var prize=Math.min(unit*carry,pot);win[top[0]]+=prize;d[top[0]]+=prize;pot-=prize;last[top[0]]=prize;ev.unshift(nameOf(top[0])+" 상금"+(carry>1?"(×"+carry+")":""));carry=1;}
        else{carry++;ev.unshift("동타 이월 → 다음 홀 ×"+carry);}
        a.forEach(function(i){if(st[i]>par-1)return;var tg=null;a.forEach(function(j){if(j!==i&&win[j]>0&&(tg==null||win[j]>win[tg]))tg=j;});
          if(tg!=null){var amt=Math.min(unit,win[tg]);win[tg]-=amt;d[tg]-=amt;win[i]+=amt;d[i]+=amt;ev.push(nameOf(i)+" 버디 → "+nameOf(tg)+"에게서 뺏음");}});
        tag=ev.join(" · ");
      }
      played++;
      var keepWin=G.id==="jopok"||G.id==="happy"||G.id==="vegas"||(G.id==="hussein"&&g.hzMode==="pot");
      if(!keepWin)a.forEach(function(i){tot[i]+=d[i];});
      var cum={};a.forEach(function(i){cum[i]=keepWin?win[i]:tot[i];});
      per[h]={d:d,cum:cum,tag:tag,pot:(G.id==="jopok"||(G.id==="hussein"&&g.hzMode==="pot"))?pot:null};
    }
    if(G.id==="jopok"||(G.id==="hussein"&&g.hzMode==="pot")){var share=Math.floor(pot/a.length);a.forEach(function(i){tot[i]=win[i]+share-seed;});extra={pot:pot,share:share,rest:pot-share*a.length,seed:seed};}
    if(G.id==="happy"||G.id==="vegas"){var paid=0;a.forEach(function(i){tot[i]=win[i]-seed;paid+=win[i];});extra={seed:seed,left:seed*a.length-paid,fund:fund,bank:bank,np:np,nearNeed:nearNeed};}
    return {tot:tot,unit:unit,played:played,skipped:skipped,need:need,doubled:doubled,act:a,per:per,game:G,extra:extra,offH:offH};
  }
  function won(v){return (v>0?"+":(v<0?"−":""))+Math.abs(v).toLocaleString("ko-KR")+"원";}
  function money(v){return Math.abs(v).toLocaleString("ko-KR")+"원";}
  function betReady(){var G=gameOf(S.bet.game);return S.bet.on&&G.ready!==false&&S.age19&&((G.id==="vegas"&&S.bet.drawMode==="off")||(amtFields(G.id)||[]).every(function(f){return amt(f[0])>0;}))&&(G.id!=="happy"||happyUnit()>0);}
  function ruleText(){var t=ruleText0();if(!S.bet.on)return t;var L=[[0,"첫 홀"],[17,"마지막 홀"]].map(function(x){var m=holeMode(x[0]);return m==="off"?x[1]+" 내기 안 함":(m==="birdie"?x[1]+" 버디피만":null);}).filter(Boolean);return t+(L.length?" · "+L.join(" · "):"");}
  function ruleText0(){
    if(!S.bet.on)return "내기 안 함";
    var g=S.bet,G=gameOf(g.game),u=amt("unit")>0?money(amt("unit")):"금액 미정",sd=amt("seed")>0?money(amt("seed")):"미정";
    if(G.ready===false)return G.name+" (규칙 받는 중)";
    if(G.id==="tadang"){var bpo=g.bp||{},on=[["tie3","3명 동타"],["tie4","4명 동타"],["triple","트리플"],["par3dbl","파3 더블"],["birdie","버디 이상"]].filter(function(x){return bpo[x[0]];}).map(function(x){return x[1];});
      return "타당 "+u+" · 배판 "+(on.length?on.join("·"):"없음")+(function(){var L=BK.map(function(x){var b=(g.bonus||{})[x[0]];if(!b||b.mode==="none")return null;return x[1]+" "+(b.mode==="stroke"?(+b.v||0)+"타":money(+b.v||0));}).filter(Boolean);return L.length?" · "+L.join("·"):"";})()+(on.length?" · "+(g.bpWhen==="this"?"당홀":"다음 홀")+" 배판 · 땅 겹치면 "+(g.stack==="cap2"?"2배까지":"4배까지"):"");}
    if(G.id==="scratch")return "스크래치 한 타 "+u;
    if(G.id==="vegas"&&g.drawMode==="off")return "뽑기(한국식) · 오프라인 진행";
    if(G.id==="vegas")return "뽑기(한국식) 1인 "+sd+" · 비기면 "+({carry:"이월 적립",next:"다음 홀 2배",none:"배판 없음"}[g.vtie||"carry"])+" · 조커 "+({bogey:"무조건 보기",four:"무조건 4타",par:"무조건 파"}[g.joker||"bogey"])+" · 니어 없으면 "+(g.nearMode==="fund"?"공금":"이월");
    if(G.id==="hussein")return g.hzMode==="pot"?"후세인(판돈) 1인 "+sd+" · 홀 상금 "+u+" · "+hzRule():"후세인 홀당 "+u+" · "+hzRule();
    if(G.id==="happy")return "해피(4321) 1인 "+sd+" · 니어 없으면 "+(g.nearMode==="fund"?"공금":"이월");
    if(G.id==="jopok")return "조폭 판돈 1인 "+sd+" · 홀 상금 "+u;
    return G.name;
  }

  /* ---------- 그리기: 다음 라운드 ---------- */
  var MOVES=[["self","각자 가요","따로 출발"],["drive","내가 태워요","픽업"],["ride","얻어 타요","누가 태워 줌"]];
  var GOPT={bpWhen:[["this","당홀"],["next","다음 홀"]],stack:[["cap2","2배까지"],["cap4","4배까지"]],hrank:[[2,"앞 홀 2등"],["turn","타순대로 돌아가며"],["manual","직접 고르기"]],nearMode:[["carry","다음 파3로 이월"],["fund","공금으로"]],hzMode:[["direct","홀마다 주고받기"],["pot","판돈 걷기"]],joker:[["bogey","무조건 보기"],["four","무조건 4타"],["par","무조건 파"]],drawMode:[["app","휴대폰으로 뽑기"],["manual","뽑기통 · 결과만 입력"],["off","오프라인"]],vtie:[["carry","이월 적립"],["next","비긴 만큼 다음 홀 2배"],["none","배판 없음"]]};
  function norm(x){return String(x||"").replace(/\s+/g,"").toLowerCase();}
  function clubById(id){var c=id==null?null:COURSES[id];return c&&c.name===S.course?c:null;}
  function recent(){try{var a=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(RKEY)||"[]");return Array.isArray(a)?a:[];}catch(e){return [];}}
  function pushRecent(name){try{var a=recent().filter(function(n){return n!==name;});a.unshift(name);localStorage.setItem(RKEY,JSON.stringify(a.slice(0,5)));}catch(e){}}
  var pickRegion=null,pickCity=null;
  function cityKey(c){var t=String(c||"").split(" ")[0];return t.length>2?t.replace(/[시군]$/,""):t.replace(/시$/,"");}
  function clubBtn(c,i){var n=c.courses&&c.courses.length?c.courses.length*9+"홀"+(c.part?" · 일부만":"")+(c.chk?" · 파 확인 필요":""):"파 직접 입력";return '<button type="button" data-club="'+i+'">'+esc(c.name)+'<span>'+esc(c.city||"")+" · "+n+'</span></button>';}
  function renderSearch(){
    var q=norm($("cq").value),box=$("cResults");
    if(!q){box.innerHTML="";return;}
    var hits=[];COURSES.forEach(function(c,i){var hay=norm(c.name+" "+(c.aliases||[]).join(" ")+" "+(c.city||""));if(hay.indexOf(q)>=0)hits.push(i);});
    box.innerHTML=hits.length?hits.slice(0,8).map(function(i){return clubBtn(COURSES[i],i);}).join(""):'<p class="note">"'+esc($("cq").value)+'" 골프장을 아직 못 찾았어요. 아래에 이름을 직접 적어도 돼요.</p>';
  }
  function renderPicker(){
    var c=S.courseId!=null?clubById(S.courseId):null;
    var label=S.course?esc(S.course):"골프장 고르기";
    var sub=c&&S.nines?esc(c.courses.length===1?"9홀 두 바퀴":c.courses[S.nines[0]].name+" → "+c.courses[S.nines[1]].name):(c&&c.courses.length?esc(c.courses[0].name)+" + 후반 직접":(S.course?"직접 입력":"검색 · 지역"));
    $("courseBtn").innerHTML=label+'<span>'+sub+' ▾</span>';
    var open=!$("picker").hidden;$("courseBtn").setAttribute("aria-expanded",String(open));
    var nb=$("nineBox");
    if(c&&c.courses&&c.courses.length>2&&S.nines){
      nb.innerHTML='<span class="note">전반</span><div class="quick">'+c.courses.map(function(x,k){return '<button type="button" class="chip" data-nine="0:'+k+'" aria-pressed="'+(S.nines[0]===k)+'">'+esc(x.name)+'</button>';}).join("")+'</div><span class="note">후반</span><div class="quick">'+c.courses.map(function(x,k){return '<button type="button" class="chip" data-nine="1:'+k+'" aria-pressed="'+(S.nines[1]===k)+'">'+esc(x.name)+'</button>';}).join("")+'</div>';
    }else if(c&&c.courses&&c.courses.length===2&&S.nines){var A=c.courses[0].name,B=c.courses[1].name;
      nb.innerHTML='<span class="note">출발 코스</span><div class="quick"><button type="button" class="chip" data-ord="0" aria-pressed="'+(S.nines[0]===0)+'">'+esc(A)+' → '+esc(B)+'</button><button type="button" class="chip" data-ord="1" aria-pressed="'+(S.nines[0]===1)+'">'+esc(B)+' → '+esc(A)+'</button></div>';
    }else nb.innerHTML="";
    if(c&&!(c.courses&&c.courses.length))nb.innerHTML+='<p class="note">이 골프장은 파를 직접 넣어요. 스코어 화면에서 홀마다 파를 눌러 맞춰 주세요.</p>';
    else{
      if(c&&c.part)nb.innerHTML+='<p class="note">'+(S.nines?'이 골프장은 일부 코스 파만 있어요. 없는 코스는 스코어 화면에서 맞춰 주세요.':'전반 '+esc(c.courses[0].name)+' 코스 파만 있어요. 후반은 스코어 화면에서 맞춰 주세요.')+'</p>';

  function chooseClub(i){
    var c=COURSES[i];S.course=c.name;S.courseId=i;
    if(c.part&&c.courses&&c.courses.length===1){S.nines=null;S.par=c.courses[0].par.concat(DEFAULT_PAR.slice(9));}
    else if(c.courses&&c.courses.length>=2){S.nines=[0,1];S.par=c.courses[0].par.concat(c.courses[1].par);}
    else if(c.courses&&c.courses.length===1){S.nines=[0,0];S.par=c.courses[0].par.concat(c.courses[0].par);}
    else{S.nines=null;S.par=DEFAULT_PAR.slice();}
    pushRecent(c.name);$("picker").hidden=true;$("cq").value="";$("cResults").innerHTML="";changed();reveal("courseBtn");
  }

  function renderGo(){
    var p=plan();
    $("depart").firstChild.nodeValue=fmt(p.depart);$("dayTag").textContent=p.prev?"전날":"";

    $("goSum").innerHTML="<b>"+fmt(p.tee)+"</b> 티오프 · <b>"+fmt(p.arrive)+"</b> 도착"+(p.pk.length?" · 픽업 "+p.pk.length+"명":"")+(so.length?" · "+esc(so.join(", "))+" 들름":"");
    $("navNaver").href=naver(S.course||"골프장");

      var link="";
      if(e.k==="stop"){var q=S.stops[e.d.id].where==="course"?(S.course+" 근처 "+e.d.q):e.d.q;link=' · <a href="'+kakao(q)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">'+e.d.name+' 찾기</a>';}
      if(e.k==="pick"&&e.x.place)link=' · <a href="'+kakao(e.x.place)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">위치 보기</a>';
      if(e.k==="arrive"&&S.course)link=' · <a href="'+kakao(S.course)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">위치 보기</a>';
      return '<li class="'+e.k+'"><div class="t">'+fmt(e.t)+'</div><div class="dot"><i></i></div><div class="what"><b>'+esc(e.title)+'</b><span>'+esc(e.note)+'</span>'+link+'</div></li>';
    }).join("");
    if(!S.tee){$("depart").firstChild.nodeValue="--:--";$("dayTag").textContent="";$("goSum").textContent="티오프 시간을 넣으면 출발 시간이 나와요.";$("timeline").innerHTML="";}

    $("driveOut").textContent=S.drive+"분";

    $("pickups").innerHTML=p.pk.length?p.pk.map(function(x){
      return '<div class="pk"><b>'+esc(nameOf(x.i))+'</b><input type="text" data-place="'+x.i+'" id="place'+x.i+'" placeholder="만날 곳 (아파트 정문 등)" value="'+esc(x.place)+'"><span class="note">경로가 몇 분 늘어나나요 · 백 싣기 3분은 자동</span><div class="quick">'+EXTRAS.map(function(v){return '<button type="button" class="chip" data-ex="'+x.i+':'+v+'" aria-pressed="'+(x.extra===v)+'">+'+v+'분</button>';}).join("")+'</div></div>';
    }).join("")+(p.pk.length>=3?'<p class="note">4명이 한 차면 골프백 4개가 트렁크에 다 안 들어갈 수 있어요.</p>':''):'<p class="note">"다음 라운드"에서 태울 사람을 고르세요.</p>';
    $("stops").innerHTML=STOPS.map(function(d){var st=S.stops[d.id];return '<div class="item"><input type="checkbox" id="st-'+d.id+'" data-stop="'+d.id+'"'+(st.on?" checked":"")+'><label class="nm" for="st-'+d.id+'">'+d.name+'<span>'+(d.sub?d.sub+" · ":"")+d.stay+'분</span></label><select data-where="'+d.id+'" aria-label="'+d.name+' 위치"'+(st.on?"":" disabled")+'>'+(d.id==="meal"?[["inside","골프장 안"],["course","골프장 근처 맛집"]]:[["home","집 근처"],["course","골프장 근처"]]).map(function(o){return '<option value="'+o[0]+'"'+(st.where===o[0]?" selected":"")+'>'+o[1]+'</option>';}).join("")+'</select></div>';}).join("");
  }
  function goText(){
    var p=plan(),so=p.ev.filter(function(e){return e.k==="stop";}).map(function(e){return e.d.name;});
    var L=[(S.course||"골프장")+" "+fmt(p.tee)+" 티오프",fmt(p.depart)+" 출발"+(so.length?" ("+so.join(", ")+" 들름)":"")+" → "+fmt(p.arrive)+" 도착 예정"];
    p.ev.filter(function(e){return e.k==="pick";}).forEach(function(e){L.push("· "+nameOf(e.x.i)+" "+fmt(e.t)+(e.x.place?" "+e.x.place:"")+" 픽업");});
    if(p.pk.length)L.push("시간 맞춰 나와 계세요");
    return L.join("\n");
  }

  /* ---------- 그리기: 스코어·정산 ---------- */
  function term(d,par,v){if(v===1)return "홀인원";if(v>=par*2)return "양파";var t={"-3":"알바트로스","-2":"이글","-1":"버디","0":"파","1":"보기","2":"더블 보기","3":"트리플 보기"};return t[String(d)]||("+"+d);}
  function renderScore(){

(공금 '+money(r.extra.fund)+' 포함)':'')+(r.extra.bank?' · 아직 안 준 니어 상금 '+money(r.extra.bank):'')+(r.extra.nearNeed.length?' · 니어 안 고른 '+r.extra.nearNeed.join(", ")+'번 홀':'')+'.':(G.id==="jopok"||(G.id==="hussein"&&S.bet.hzMode==="pot"))?' 판돈 1인 '+money(r.extra.seed)+'을 빼고, 남은 판돈 '+money(r.extra.pot)+'을 '+r.act.length+'명이 '+money(r.extra.share)+'씩 나눈 결과예요'+(r.extra.rest?' (나머지 '+money(r.extra.rest)+'은 따로)':'')+'.':'';

    $("parset").innerHTML=S.par.map(function(p,k){return '<button type="button" data-ph="'+k+'" aria-label="'+(k+1)+'번 홀 파 '+p+'"><span>'+(k+1)+'</span>'+p+'</button>';}).join("");
    $("parTotal").textContent="합계 파 "+S.par.reduce(function(x,y){return x+y;},0)+(S.course?" · "+S.course:"");
  }
  function scoreText(){
    var a=act(),list=a.map(function(i){return {i:i,s:stats(i)};}).sort(function(x,y){return x.s.vs-y.s.vs;});
    var L=["[스코어] "+(S.course||"골프장")+" "+(S.date||"")],mx=0;
    list.forEach(function(x){mx=Math.max(mx,x.s.thru);L.push(nameOf(x.i)+" "+(x.s.thru?vp(x.s.vs)+" ("+x.s.tot+"타)":"기록 없음"));});
    if(mx<18)L.splice(1,0,mx+"홀까지");
    var r=betReady()?calc():null;
    if(r&&!r.bad&&r.played){L.push("");L.push("[정산] "+ruleText());r.act.slice().sort(function(x,y){return r.tot[y]-r.tot[x];}).forEach(function(i){L.push(nameOf(i)+" "+won(r.tot[i]));});if(r.doubled.length)L.push("배판: "+r.doubled.join(", "));}
    L.push("— 슬기로운 라운드(시험판)");return L.join("\n");
  }

  /* ---------- 탭 ---------- */
  function showTab(t){
    S.tab=t;save();
    ["plan","go","score","type"].forEach(function(x){$("view-"+x).hidden=x!==t;var tb=$("tab-"+x);if(tb)tb.setAttribute("aria-selected",String(x===t));});
    if(t==="type"&&window.GRAS_OPEN)window.GRAS_OPEN();
    try{history.replaceState(null,"","#"+t);}catch(e){}
    window.scrollTo(0,0);
  }
  function renderLevel(){var lv=getLevel();$("levelAsk").hidden=!!lv;if(!lv)$("levelChips").innerHTML=LEVELS.map(function(l){return '<button type="button" class="chip" data-level="'+l[0]+'">'+l[1]+' <span class="note">'+l[2]+'</span></button>';}).join("");}
  function render(){renderLevel();$("sampleBar").hidden=!S.sample;renderPlan();renderGo();renderScore();}
  function changed(){S.sample=false;save();render();}

  document.addEventListener("click",function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest("button");if(!b)return;var d=b.dataset;
    if(d.tab){showTab(d.tab);return;}
    if(d.level){setLevel(d.level);render();toast("준비물을 "+LEVELS.filter(function(l){return l[0]===d.level;})[0][1]+" 기준으로 맞췄어요");return;}
    if(d.club!=null){chooseClub(+d.club);return;}
    if(d.region){pickRegion=pickRegion===d.region?null:d.region;pickCity=null;renderPicker();if(pickRegion)reveal($("cityBox").children.length?"cityBox":"regionList");return;}
    if(d.city){pickCity=pickCity===d.city?null:d.city;renderPicker();if(pickCity)reveal("regionList");return;}
    if(d.recent){var ri=-1;COURSES.forEach(function(c,i){if(c.name===d.recent)ri=i;});if(ri>=0)chooseClub(ri);else{S.course=d.recent;S.courseId=null;S.nines=null;$("picker").hidden=true;changed();}return;}
    if(d.ord!=null){var oc=clubById(S.courseId);if(oc&&oc.courses.length===2){S.nines=d.ord==="1"?[1,0]:[0,1];S.par=oc.courses[S.nines[0]].par.concat(oc.courses[S.nines[1]].par);changed();}return;}
    if(d.nine){var np=d.nine.split(":"),cc=clubById(S.courseId);if(cc){S.nines[+np[0]]=+np[1];S.par=cc.courses[S.nines[0]].par.concat(cc.courses[S.nines[1]].par);changed();}return;}
    if(d.act==="togglePicker"){$("picker").hidden=!$("picker").hidden;renderPicker();return;}
    if(d.move){S.move=d.move;changed();return;}
    if(d.game){if(d.game==="off")S.bet.on=false;else{S.bet.on=true;S.bet.game=d.game;}changed();return;}
    if(d.skip){var sp=d.skip.split(":");S.bet.skip=Object.assign({first:"on",last:"on"},S.bet.skip);S.bet.skip[sp[0]]=sp[1];changed();return;}
    if(d.gk){S.bet[d.gk]=d.gk==="hrank"&&!isNaN(+d.gv)?+d.gv:d.gv;changed();return;}
    if(d.bm){var bm=d.bm.split(":");S.bet.bonus=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(S.bet.bonus||{}));var cur=S.bet.bonus[bm[0]]||{v:0};var MUL={birdie:1,putt:3,shot:5,hio:10},bb0=S.bet.bonus.birdie||{},base=bb0.mode==="cash"&&+bb0.v>0?+bb0.v:10000,dv=[MUL[bm[0]],bm[0]==="birdie"?10000:base*MUL[bm[0]]];
      if(bm[1]!==cur.mode)cur={mode:bm[1],v:bm[1]==="cash"?dv[1]:(bm[1]==="stroke"?dv[0]:cur.v)};S.bet.bonus[bm[0]]=cur;
      if(bm[0]==="birdie"&&bm[1]!=="none"){["putt","shot","hio"].forEach(function(k){S.bet.bonus[k]={mode:bm[1],v:bm[1]==="cash"?(+cur.v||10000)*MUL[k]:MUL[k]};});}
      changed();return;}
    if(d.eg){var ep=d.eg.split(":");S.bet.eg[ep[0]]=ep[1];changed();return;}
    if(d.bp){S.bet.bp=Object.assign({},S.bet.bp||{});S.bet.bp[d.bp]=!S.bet.bp[d.bp];changed();return;}
    if(d.vg){var vp2=d.vg.split(":");S.bet.vg[vp2[0]]=+vp2[1];changed();return;}
    if(d.ord!=null){var a5=act(),dr=(S.bet.orderDraft||[]).slice();if(dr.indexOf(+d.ord)<0)dr.push(+d.ord);if(dr.length>=a5.length-1){a5.forEach(function(i){if(dr.indexOf(i)<0)dr.push(i);});S.bet.order=dr;S.bet.orderDraft=null;toast("타순: "+dr.map(nameOf).join(" → "));}else S.bet.orderDraft=dr;changed();return;}
    if(d.stick){var sp=d.stick.split(":"),hh=+sp[0],k=+sp[1],a6=act();S.bet.dr=Object.assign({},S.bet.dr||{});var dd=S.bet.dr[hh]?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(S.bet.dr[hh])):null;
      if(!dd){var dk=["A","A","B","B","J"];for(var z4=dk.length-1;z4>0;z4--){var r4=Math.floor(Math.random()*(z4+1)),t4=dk[z4];dk[z4]=dk[r4];dk[r4]=t4;}dd={deck:dk,picks:{}};}
      var taken=Object.keys(dd.picks).map(function(x){return dd.picks[x];});if(taken.indexOf(k)>=0)return;
      var tn=null;for(var z5=0;z5<a6.length;z5++)if(dd.picks[a6[z5]]==null){tn=a6[z5];break;}if(tn==null)return;
      dd.picks[tn]=k;S.bet.dr[hh]=dd;
      if(Object.keys(dd.picks).length>=a6.length){var m5={};a6.forEach(function(i){m5[i]=dd.deck[dd.picks[i]];});S.bet.pk=Object.assign({},S.bet.pk||{});S.bet.pk[hh]=m5;changed();var tm5=sticksOf(hh,a6);if(tm5)toast("팀: "+tm5.map(function(t){return t.map(function(i){return nameOf(i)+(m5[i]==="J"?"(조커)":"");}).join("·");}).join(" vs "));return;}
      changed();return;}
    if(d.pk){var pp=d.pk.split(":");S.bet.pk=Object.assign({},S.bet.pk||{});S.bet.pk[pp[0]]=Object.assign({},S.bet.pk[pp[0]]||{});S.bet.pk[pp[0]][pp[1]]=pp[2];changed();return;}
    if(d.amtq){var aq=d.amtq.split(":"),gq=S.bet.game;S.bet.amt[gq]=Object.assign({},S.bet.amt[gq]||{});S.bet.amt[gq][aq[0]]=+aq[1];changed();return;}
    if(d.near){var nr=d.near.split(":");S.bet.near[nr[0]]=+nr[1];changed();return;}
    if(d.stk){S.bet.stack=d.stk;changed();return;}
    if(d.gal!=null&&d.gal!==""){var gq=d.gal.split(":");S.bet.gal=setGal(S.bet.gal,+gq[0],+gq[1]);changed();return;}
    if(d.ab!=null&&d.ab!==""){S.bet.ab=Object.assign({},S.bet.ab||{});S.bet.ab[d.ab]=togIv(S.bet.ab[d.ab],S.hole);changed();return;}
    if(d.dc){var dq2=d.dc.split(":");S.bet.dc=Object.assign({},S.bet.dc||{});if(S.bet.dc[dq2[0]]!=null&&+S.bet.dc[dq2[0]]===+dq2[1])delete S.bet.dc[dq2[0]];else S.bet.dc[dq2[0]]=+dq2[1];changed();return;}
    if(d.hz){var hp=d.hz.split(":");S.bet.hz[hp[0]]=+hp[1];changed();return;}
    if(d.buf){S.buffer=+d.buf;changed();return;}
    if(d.tr!=null){S.traffic=+d.tr;changed();return;}
    if(d.ex){var pr=d.ex.split(":");S.pick[pr[0]]=Object.assign({on:true},S.pick[pr[0]]||{},{extra:+pr[1]});changed();return;}
    if(d.sv){var sv=d.sv.split(":"),si=+sv[0],sd=+sv[1],hs=S.hole,nv3=S.par[hs]+sd;S.scores[si][hs]=S.scores[si][hs]===nv3?null:nv3;changed();return;}
    if(d.p!=null){var i=+d.p,h=S.hole,cur=S.scores[i][h],nv=cur==null?S.par[h]+(+d.d>0?0:-1):cur+(+d.d);S.scores[i][h]=Math.max(1,Math.min(15,nv));changed();return;}
    if(d.par){S.par[S.hole]=+d.par;changed();return;}
    if(d.ph!=null){var ph=+d.ph;S.par[ph]=S.par[ph]>=5?3:S.par[ph]+1;changed();return;}
    if(d.h!=null){S.hole=+d.h;save();renderScore();return;}
    switch(d.act){
      case "prev":if(S.hole>0){S.hole--;save();renderScore();}return;
      case "next":if(S.hole<17){S.hole++;save();renderScore();}return;
      case "drawOrder":var o2=act().slice();for(var z=o2.length-1;z>0;z--){var r2=Math.floor(Math.random()*(z+1)),t2=o2[z];o2[z]=o2[r2];o2[r2]=t2;}S.bet.order=o2;S.bet.orderDraft=null;changed();toast("타순: "+o2.map(nameOf).join(" → "));return;
      case "redraw":S.bet.dr=Object.assign({},S.bet.dr||{});delete S.bet.dr[S.hole];S.bet.pk=Object.assign({},S.bet.pk||{});delete S.bet.pk[S.hole];changed();return;
      case "drawSticks":var stk=["A","A","B","B","J"];for(var z2=stk.length-1;z2>0;z2--){var r3=Math.floor(Math.random()*(z2+1)),t3=stk[z2];stk[z2]=stk[r3];stk[r3]=t3;}S.bet.pk=Object.assign({},S.bet.pk||{});var m3={};act().forEach(function(i,k){m3[i]=stk[k];});S.bet.pk[S.hole]=m3;changed();return;
      case "ucSet":(function(){var el=$("ucIn"),v=el?Math.round(+el.value):NaN;if(!(v>0)){toast("바꿀 한 타 금액을 넣어 주세요");return;}var hh=S.hole,uc=Object.assign({},S.bet.uc||{});delete uc[hh];S.bet.uc=uc;var prev=unitAt(hh,amt("unit"));if(v!==prev)uc[hh]=v;S.bet.uc=uc;changed();toast((hh+1)+"번 홀부터 한 타 "+money(v));})();return;
      case "ucDel":(function(){var uc=Object.assign({},S.bet.uc||{});delete uc[S.hole];S.bet.uc=uc;changed();})();return;
      case "fillPar":act().forEach(function(i){if(S.scores[i][S.hole]==null)S.scores[i][S.hole]=S.par[S.hole];});changed();return;
      case "driveMinus":S.drive=Math.max(5,S.drive-5);changed();return;
      case "drivePlus":S.drive=Math.min(300,S.drive+5);changed();return;
      case "copyGo":copy(goText(),"goCopy","문구를 복사했어요. 단톡방에 붙여넣으세요.");return;
      case "copyScore":copy(scoreText(),"scoreCopy","결과를 복사했어요. 단톡방에 붙여넣으세요.");return;
      case "askNew":$("resetArea").innerHTML='<div class="confirm"><span>지금 라운드 기록이 지워져요.</span><button class="btn btn-plain" type="button" data-act="new">지우고 새로 시작</button><button class="btn btn-plain" type="button" data-act="cancelNew">취소</button></div>';return;
      case "cancelNew":$("resetArea").innerHTML='<button class="btn btn-plain" type="button" data-act="askNew">새 라운드 시작</button>';return;
      case "new":
        var keep=S.sample?null:{players:S.players.slice(),bet:Object.assign({},S.bet),stops:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(S.stops)),drive:S.drive,first:false};
        S=blank();if(keep){S.players=keep.players;S.bet=keep.bet;S.bet.vg={};S.bet.hz={};S.bet.near={};S.bet.order=null;S.bet.eg={};S.bet.pk={};S.bet.dr={};S.bet.dc={};S.bet.ab={};S.bet.gal=[];S.bet.uc={};S.stops=keep.stops;S.drive=keep.drive;}
        $("resetArea").innerHTML='<button class="btn btn-plain" type="button" data-act="askNew">새 라운드 시작</button>';
        save();render();showTab("plan");toast("새 라운드를 시작해요. 공치러 어디 가세요?");return;
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener("input",function(e){
    var t=e.target,d=t.dataset||{};
    if(t.id==="course"){S.course=t.value;S.courseId=null;S.nines=null;changed();return;}
    if(t.id==="cq"){renderSearch();return;}
    if(t.id==="date"){S.date=t.value;changed();return;}
    if(t.id==="tee"){if(t.value){S.tee=t.value;changed();}return;}
    if(d.pn!=null){S.players[+d.pn]=t.value;changed();return;}
    if(d.bv){S.bet.bonus=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(S.bet.bonus||{}));var nv2=Math.max(0,Math.floor(+t.value||0)),ob3=S.bet.bonus[d.bv]||{};
      if(d.bv==="birdie"&&ob3.mode==="cash"){var M2={putt:3,shot:5,hio:10},old=+ob3.v||0;Object.keys(M2).forEach(function(k){var r=S.bet.bonus[k];if(r&&r.mode==="cash"&&(+r.v===old*M2[k]||!(+r.v)))r.v=nv2*M2[k];});}
      S.bet.bonus[d.bv]=Object.assign({mode:"stroke"},ob3,{v:nv2});changed();return;}
    if(d.amt){var gid=S.bet.game;S.bet.amt[gid]=Object.assign({},S.bet.amt[gid]||{});S.bet.amt[gid][d.amt]=Math.max(0,Math.floor(+t.value||0));changed();return;}
    if(d.place!=null){S.pick[d.place]=Object.assign({on:true,extra:10},S.pick[d.place]||{},{place:t.value});S.sample=false;save();renderPlan();return;}
  });
  document.addEventListener("change",function(e){
    var t=e.target,d=t.dataset||{};
    if(t.id==="age19"){S.age19=t.checked;changed();return;}
    if(t.id==="first"){S.first=t.checked;if(S.first&&S.buffer<50)S.buffer=50;changed();return;}
    if(d.pick!=null){S.pick[d.pick]=Object.assign({extra:10,place:""},S.pick[d.pick]||{},{on:t.checked});changed();return;}
    if(d.stop){S.stops[d.stop].on=t.checked;changed();return;}
    if(d.where){S.stops[d.where].where=t.value;changed();return;}
    if(d.pack!=null){S.pack[PACK()[+d.pack]]=t.checked;save();return;}
  });

  render();
  var h0=(location.hash||"").slice(1);showTab(["plan","go","score","type"].indexOf(h0)>=0?h0:"plan");
})();
</script>
</body></html>
